=== BUGGY CODE ===
class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        #Use Kahn's algorithm of toposort using a queue and bfs!
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n
        
        #1st step: build adjacency list grpah and update the initial indegrees of every node!
        for edge in edges:
            src, dest = edge[0], edge[1]
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1
        
        
        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]
        #2nd step: go through the indegrees array and add to queue for any node that has no ancestor!
        for i in range(len(indegrees)):
            if(indegrees[i] == 0):
                queue.append(i)
        
        #Kahn's algorithm initiation!
        while queue:
            cur = queue.pop()
            
            #for each neighbor
            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                #current node is ancestor to each and every neighboring node!
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                #every ancestor of current node is also an ancestor to the neighboring node!
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])
                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if(indegrees[neighbor] == 0):
                    queue.append(neighbor)
        
        #at the end, we should have set of ancestors for each and every node!
        #in worst case, set s for ith node could have all other vertices be ancestor to node i !
        ans = [(sorted(list(s))) for s in myList]
        return ans

Observe the following faulty python code. It contains one or more bugs.

```python
class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        #Use Kahn's algorithm of toposort using a queue and bfs!
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n
        
        #1st step: build adjacency list grpah and update the initial indegrees of every node!
        for edge in edges:
            src, dest = edge[0], edge[1]
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1
        
        
        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]
        #2nd step: go through the indegrees array and add to queue for any node that has no ancestor!
        for i in range(len(indegrees)):
            if(indegrees[i] == 0):
                queue.append(i)
        
        #Kahn's algorithm initiation!
        while queue:
            cur = queue.pop()
            
            #for each neighbor
            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                #current node is ancestor to each and every neighboring node!
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                #every ancestor of current node is also an ancestor to the neighboring node!
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])
                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if(indegrees[neighbor] == 0):
                    queue.append(neighbor)
        
        #at the end, we should have set of ancestors for each and every node!
        #in worst case, set s for ith node could have all other vertices be ancestor to node i !
        ans = [(sorted(list(s))) for s in myList]
        return ans
```

- After the edit, rerun that path or the most relevant edge case.
- Then do one small coupled-region scan: a directly connected guard, index update, return path, or call site that could still break the same fix.
- If the recheck is still off, refine the same local region before opening a distant new theory.
- Once the failing path and its directly coupled region look consistent, finalize the code.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- staying in diagnosis mode for too long without testing the next plausible edit;
- moving from a local repair into a fresh branch of speculative causes when the current path has not been rechecked yet;
- turning a focused fix into a broad rewrite unless the surrounding code clearly forces it.

Original code:

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        #Use Kahn's algorithm of toposort using a queue and bfs!
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n
        
        for edge in edges:
            src, dest = edge[0], edge[1]
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1
        
        
        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]
        #2nd step: go through the indegrees array and add to queue for any node that has no ancestor!
        for i in range(len(indegrees)):
            if(indegrees[i] == 0):
                queue.append(i)
        
        #Kahn's algorithm initiation!
        while queue:
            cur = queue.pop()
            
            #for each neighbor
            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                #current node is ancestor to each and every neighboring node!
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                #every ancestor of current node is also an ancestor to the neighboring node!
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])
                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if(indegrees[neighbor] == 0):
                    queue.append(neighbor)
        
        #at the end, we should have set of ancestors for each and every node!
        #in worst case, set s for ith node could have all other vertices be ancestor to node i !
        ans = [(sorted(list(s))) for s in myList]
        return ans

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:

            indegrees[dest] += 1

                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):

                queue.append(i)

                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:

            indegrees[dest] += 1

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:

            indegrees[dest] += 1

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

Therefore, at the time a node is enqueued, all its incoming edges have been processed. The source of each incoming edge has been processed. By induction, all ancestors of those sources have been processed and propagated. Thus ans[node] complete. Good.

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

        indegrees = [0] * n

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

Therefore, at the time a node is enqueued, all its incoming edges have been processed. The source of each incoming edge has been processed. By induction, all ancestors of those sources have been processed and propagated. Thus ans[node] complete. Good.

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]

We already proved no. But let's formalize: In Kahn's algorithm, a node is enqueued only when indegree becomes 0. Indegree is decremented only when an incoming edge is processed. Therefore, at the time a node is enqueued, all its incoming edges have been processed. The source of each incoming edge has been processed. By induction, all ancestors of those sources have been processed and propagated. Thus ans[node] complete. Good.

=== RAW ANSWER ===
```python
from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List
from collections import deque

class Solution:
    def getAncestors(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]]) -> List[List[int]]:
        graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        indegrees = [0] * n

        for src, dest in edges:
            graph[src].append(dest)
            indegrees[dest] += 1

        queue = deque()
        ans = [set() for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            if indegrees[i] == 0:
                queue.append(i)

        while queue:
            cur = queue.popleft()

            for neighbor in graph[cur]:
                ans[neighbor].add(cur)
                ans[neighbor].update(ans[cur])

                indegrees[neighbor] -= 1
                if indegrees[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)

        return [sorted(s) for s in ans]
